[Director 指令] [Director 紧急指令] ⚠️ exp_esj_entity_self_judgment 严重问题，立即处理：

**1. 不要将 ESJ 标记为 done/completed。** Worker 返回 status=done 但实验实际零执行——这是虚假完成信号。Registry 仍为 ALLOCATED，保持原样。

**2. Worker 连错了服务器。** Registry 分配的是 autodl-5090-c cuda:1，Worker 却连到了 autodl-5090（被 32B 训练占满）。autodl-5090-c cuda:1 当前完全空闲（0 MiB, 0% util）。

**3. 重新执行 ESJ 实验，步骤如下：**
- 连接 **autodl-5090-c**（不是 autodl-5090）
- 检查 autodl-5090-c 上是否有 ESJ 所需的代码（run_esj.py）、FewNERD 数据、模型 checkpoint。目前 run_esj.py 只在 autodl-5090 上，需要同步到 autodl-5090-c
- 使用 **cuda:1**
- 模型问题：原计划 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，Worker 之前自行换成了 Qwen3-8B 未经批准。先检查 autodl-5090-c 上有哪些可用模型，汇报给我决定用哪个，**不要自行替换模型**

优先级最高，插队处理。

Well-separated temperature mixing (T=0.7+T=1.0) achieves Pareto optimality for both uniform and VC construction, while VC-weighted construction flattens the trade-off slope, reducing the diversity cost of quality.\", \"metrics\": {\"dataset\": \"SciERC\", \"configs\": {\"t07\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.777, \"uniform_delta\": 1.81, \"vc_delta\": 1.17, \"pareto\": \"uniform only\"}, \"t08\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.819, \"uniform_delta\": 1.25, \"vc_delta\": 1.68, \"pareto\": \"VC only\"}, \"mix_07_10\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.83, \"uniform_delta\": 1.43, \"vc_delta\": 1.67, \"pareto\": \"both\"}, \"t10\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.877, \"uniform_delta\": 0.75, \"vc_delta\": 1.21, \"pareto\": \"both (highest diversity)\"}, \"mix_08_10\": {\"one_minus_degen\": 0.789, \"uniform_delta\": -0.17, \"vc_delta\": 0.47, \"pareto\": \"dominated\"}}, \"vc_slope_reduction\": \"~50% flatter than uniform\", \"key_insight\": \"well-separated T mixing dual Pareto optimal; close T mixing dominated\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"A4 Pareto diversity-quality analysis on SciERC. Mix T=0.7+T=1.0 is dual-Pareto optimal (uniform +1.43pp, VC +1.67pp). VC construction flattens trade-off slope ~50% vs uniform — increasing diversity costs less quality. Mix T=0.8+T=1.0 is dominated (close T → higher degeneracy, no construction gain). Confirms DGEC heterogeneous-T finding: ΔT matters, ΔT=0.3 beneficial, ΔT=0.2 harmful. Pareto plot artifact registered.\"}, \"exp_adaptive_sampling_budget\": {\"status\": \"PREPARING\", \"retry_count\": 0, \"created_at\": \"2026-05-20T09:29:22.761Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-20T09:29:41.158Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Confidence-aware adaptive sampling budget allocation (fewer samples for high-confidence instances, full N=8 for low-confidence) can recover nearly all SC F1 gains while saving 40-50% of inference cost, because high-confidence instances already have low diversity and gain nothing from additional samples.\", \"metrics\": {\"model\": \"Qwen3-8B FT\", \"fewnerd\": {\"n_instances\": 37648, \"greedy_f1\": 0.7884, \"uniform_n8_f1\": 0.7819, \"best_adaptive\": \"h1_l8\", \"best_adaptive_f1\": 0.7849, \"best_delta_vs_u8\": \"+0.30pp\", \"sample_savings\": \"43.8%\", \"note\": \"Greedy > U8; adaptive h1/l8 best: greedy for high-conf, N=8 SC for low-conf\"}, \"scierc\": {\"n_instances\": 551, \"greedy_f1\": 0.6592, \"uniform_n8_f1\": 0.6712, \"best_adaptive\": \"top67%_h2_l8\", \"best_adaptive_f1\": 0.6707, \"best_delta_vs_u8\": \"-0.05pp\", \"sample_savings\": \"50.2%\", \"note\": \"U8 > Greedy; top67% h2/l8 saves 50% with negligible loss\"}}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"B7 adaptive sampling budget analysis on Qwen3-8B FT. Key findings: (1) FewNERD: greedy already beats uniform N=8 SC (+0.65pp), adaptive h1/l8 is optimal (+0.30pp vs U8, saves 43.8% samples); (2) SciERC: U8 beats greedy (+1.20pp), top67% h2/l8 saves 50% with only -0.05pp loss. Practical implication: confidence-gated sampling can halve inference cost. A learned θ predictor (input: degeneracy_level, lp_variance, N, dataset, model) can select per-instance optimal θ, closing the gap between fixed θ=0.5 and oracle θ. Leave-one-config-out CV on 16 configs from existing N=8/16 data. 5 variants: LogReg/MLP × feature subsets + degeneracy-only baseline. | — | — | — |\\n| exp_14b_inference_fewnerd | 14B Qwen2.5 inference on FewNERD (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): primary dataset should show oracle scaling law F1(N)=a-b*N^(-c) consistent at 14B scale, filling the scaling curve between 7B and 32B. | — | — | — |\\n| exp_b1_scd_threshold_ablation | Construction threshold sweep for SCD pseudo-label generation on FewNERD: θ∈{1/N, 2/N, 3/N, 0.5}. Higher θ = more conservative pseudo-labels (higher precision, lower recall). Optimal θ balances pseudo-label quality vs quantity for SCD training effectiveness. | — | — | — |\\n\\n## 待处理\\n- exp_b3_prompted_diversity: Structurally diverse prompt templates (role framing, output format variants, few-shot exemplar diversity) increase inter-sample entity extraction diversity beyond temperature-only diversity, improving construction F1 over identical-prompt N=8 sampling on FewNERD/SciERC/CoNLL.（等待人工审批）\\n- exp_b1_scd: Self-Consistency Distillation via theta2n construction pseudo-labels can teach Qwen3-8B-FT to internalize SC benefits: after iterative SCD (M0→M1→M2→M3), greedy F1 should approach N=8 construction F1 (FewNERD target: 0.764 vs current greedy 0.748). CoNLL serves as negative control (construction Δ=-0.4pp, SCD should not help). Transductive setting: pseudo-labels from test set SC outputs.（等待人工审批）\\n- exp_asb_adaptive_budget: Adaptive Sample Budget: extension of E_θ threshold optimization to sample count N. Instance features (degeneracy, LP variance, agreement rate) predict optimal N per instance. Under fixed total budget=8 (average), adaptive allocation outperforms uniform N=8 by allocating more samples to high-uncertainty instances and fewer to degenerate/easy ones.（排队等待 GPU）\\n- exp_esj_entity_self_judgment: Entity-Level Self-Judgment: F4 diagnostic proves 4/5 signals are instance-level only, LP is per-sample but compressed. ESJ provides true per-sample-per-entity signal via yes/no verification prompts. ESJ-weighted entity construction should outperform majority vote by leveraging entity-level confidence scores that structural signals cannot provide. Parent: exp_e2_mixture_of_signals (F4 LP-only diagnostic).（已分配 autodl-5090-c GPU:cuda:1，等待提交）\\n- exp_lsc_latent_sc: Latent Self-Consistency: F4 diagnostic proves LP is compressed (median range 0.041 nats). Hidden states contain richer per-sample information than scalar LP. Entity-level mean-pooled hidden state representations enable latent aggregation (centroid selection, latent confidence scoring, clustering) that bypasses LP compression and provides discriminative per-sample ranking unavailable from output-space signals.（已分配 autodl-5090-c GPU:cuda:2，等待提交）\\n- exp_mrsc_multi_round: Multi-Round Iterative SC: N inverse scaling reveals disputed entities cause failures. Focused re-prompting on disputed entities (agreement < 0.6) with weighted aggregation (Round 2 weight=2) should reduce error rate on disputed entities without affecting non-disputed ones, closing part of the selection gap through iterative refinement rather than single-round aggregation.（已分配 autodl-5090-c GPU:cuda:3，等待提交）\\n- exp_14b_inference_conll: 14B Qwen2.5 inference on CoNLL (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): high-resource dataset should show higher degeneracy at 14B scale, consistent with 7B pattern where construction gain is negative.（准备中）\\n- exp_b2_v4_inverse_frequency: Inverse-frequency weighting of entity features (agreement_count, vc, sj normalized by log(1+type_freq)) improves cross-domain generalization by reducing dominance of high-frequency entity types. Two variants: A) replace original features, B) augment as additional features.（准备中）\\n- exp_b2_v3_domain_invariant: Removing domain-specific features (dataset_enc, entity_type distributions) forces the verifier to rely on domain-invariant signals, improving cross-domain generalization at the cost of some discriminative power. If LGB AUC improves on held-out domains, domain features were causing negative transfer.（准备中）\\n- exp_b2_v2_multi_domain_joint: Joint multi-domain training (CoNLL+SciERC+FewNERD LODO) improves entity verifier AUC and cross-domain construction F1 by exposing the model to diverse entity patterns. Tests whether training data diversity overcomes the calibration gap that caused B2 original to fail on FewNERD.（准备中）\\n- …另有 9 项\\n\\n## 近期结论\\n- **exp_14b_inference_scierc** (killed): 14B Qwen2.5 inference on SciERC (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): correlation-selection gap pattern should be consistent at 14B scale with intermediate degeneracy rates between 7B and 32B.\\n- **exp_qwen25_14b_fewnerd** (success): The correlation-selection gap pattern is consistent at 14B scale, filling the gap between 8B and 32B. Qwen2.5-14B QLoRA fine-tuned on FewNERD should show intermediate degeneracy rates and similar signal ranking patterns, providing a smooth scaling curve for the diagnostic findings.\\n- **exp_b1_scd_conll_control** (negative): Negative control: SCD on CoNLL where construction gain is negative (-0.4pp, high degeneracy 70%). If SCD learns from construction pseudo-labels, it should NOT improve greedy F1 on a dataset where construction itself hurts. Expected: no improvement or slight degradation. Validates SCD mechanism.\\n\\n## 趋势\\n成功 103 / 负向 37 / 失败 4 / 共 173\\n\\n## 下一步\\n（无活跃 idea，请通过 ideas.md 添加）\\n\\n## 资源状态\\nautodl-5090: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\\nautodl-5090-b: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\\nautodl-5090-c: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\\n\\n## 告警\\n- ft_002: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- mvp_pilot_002: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp_007_ner_llama: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp001_seed123: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp001_seed456: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp008_re_n16: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_002_merge_conll2003: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_001_n16_seed123_full: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_001_n16_seed456_full: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_008_re_n16_full: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_001_seed42_v2_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_001_seed123_v2_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_001_seed456_v2_ner_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_002_conll_n16_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_007_llama_n16_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_018_llama_scierc_seed456: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_019_supervised_verifier: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_lp_t07_scierc: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_freeform_seed789_v2: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune_v2: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune_v3: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp_qwen3_4b_conll_merge_inference_v2: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_merge_inference: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_tacred_re_benchmark: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_vc_lp_cascade_selection: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- hidden_state_probe_conll_v2: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- hidden_state_probe_conll_full: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- dgs_pilot: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- mbr_pilot: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_031_fewnerd_t12: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_030_conll_t12: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_14b_inference_scierc: 已被终止 — unknown\\n\", \"total_chars\": 7267, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

A learned θ predictor (input: degeneracy_level, lp_variance, N, dataset, model) can select per-instance optimal θ, closing the gap between fixed θ=0.5 and oracle θ. Leave-one-config-out CV on 16 configs from existing N=8/16 data. 5 variants: LogReg/MLP × feature subsets + degeneracy-only baseline. | — | — | — |\\n| exp_14b_inference_fewnerd | 14B Qwen2.5 inference on FewNERD (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): primary dataset should show oracle scaling law F1(N)=a-b*N^(-c) consistent at 14B scale, filling the scaling curve between 7B and 32B. | — | — | — |\\n| exp_b1_scd_threshold_ablation | Construction threshold sweep for SCD pseudo-label generation on FewNERD: θ∈{1/N, 2/N, 3/N, 0.5}. Higher θ = more conservative pseudo-labels (higher precision, lower recall). Optimal θ balances pseudo-label quality vs quantity for SCD training effectiveness. | — | — | — |\\n\\n## 待处理\\n- exp_b3_prompted_diversity: Structurally diverse prompt templates (role framing, output format variants, few-shot exemplar diversity) increase inter-sample entity extraction diversity beyond temperature-only diversity, improving construction F1 over identical-prompt N=8 sampling on FewNERD/SciERC/CoNLL.（等待人工审批）\\n- exp_b1_scd: Self-Consistency Distillation via theta2n construction pseudo-labels can teach Qwen3-8B-FT to internalize SC benefits: after iterative SCD (M0→M1→M2→M3), greedy F1 should approach N=8 construction F1 (FewNERD target: 0.764 vs current greedy 0.748). CoNLL serves as negative control (construction Δ=-0.4pp, SCD should not help). Transductive setting: pseudo-labels from test set SC outputs.（等待人工审批）\\n- exp_asb_adaptive_budget: Adaptive Sample Budget: extension of E_θ threshold optimization to sample count N. Instance features (degeneracy, LP variance, agreement rate) predict optimal N per instance. Under fixed total budget=8 (average), adaptive allocation outperforms uniform N=8 by allocating more samples to high-uncertainty instances and fewer to degenerate/easy ones.（排队等待 GPU）\\n- exp_esj_entity_self_judgment: Entity-Level Self-Judgment: F4 diagnostic proves 4/5 signals are instance-level only, LP is per-sample but compressed. ESJ provides true per-sample-per-entity signal via yes/no verification prompts. ESJ-weighted entity construction should outperform majority vote by leveraging entity-level confidence scores that structural signals cannot provide. Parent: exp_e2_mixture_of_signals (F4 LP-only diagnostic).（已分配 autodl-5090-c GPU:cuda:1，等待提交）\\n- exp_lsc_latent_sc: Latent Self-Consistency: F4 diagnostic proves LP is compressed (median range 0.041 nats). Hidden states contain richer per-sample information than scalar LP. Entity-level mean-pooled hidden state representations enable latent aggregation (centroid selection, latent confidence scoring, clustering) that bypasses LP compression and provides discriminative per-sample ranking unavailable from output-space signals.（已分配 autodl-5090-c GPU:cuda:2，等待提交）\\n- exp_mrsc_multi_round: Multi-Round Iterative SC: N inverse scaling reveals disputed entities cause failures. Focused re-prompting on disputed entities (agreement < 0.6) with weighted aggregation (Round 2 weight=2) should reduce error rate on disputed entities without affecting non-disputed ones, closing part of the selection gap through iterative refinement rather than single-round aggregation.（已分配 autodl-5090-c GPU:cuda:3，等待提交）\\n- exp_14b_inference_conll: 14B Qwen2.5 inference on CoNLL (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): high-resource dataset should show higher degeneracy at 14B scale, consistent with 7B pattern where construction gain is negative.（准备中）\\n- exp_b2_v4_inverse_frequency: Inverse-frequency weighting of entity features (agreement_count, vc, sj normalized by log(1+type_freq)) improves cross-domain generalization by reducing dominance of high-frequency entity types. Two variants: A) replace original features, B) augment as additional features.（准备中）\\n- exp_b2_v3_domain_invariant: Removing domain-specific features (dataset_enc, entity_type distributions) forces the verifier to rely on domain-invariant signals, improving cross-domain generalization at the cost of some discriminative power. If LGB AUC improves on held-out domains, domain features were causing negative transfer.（准备中）\\n- exp_b2_v2_multi_domain_joint: Joint multi-domain training (CoNLL+SciERC+FewNERD LODO) improves entity verifier AUC and cross-domain construction F1 by exposing the model to diverse entity patterns. Tests whether training data diversity overcomes the calibration gap that caused B2 original to fail on FewNERD.（准备中）\\n- …另有 9 项\\n\\n## 近期结论\\n- **exp_14b_inference_scierc** (killed): 14B Qwen2.5 inference on SciERC (N∈{1,2,4,8,16}): correlation-selection gap pattern should be consistent at 14B scale with intermediate degeneracy rates between 7B and 32B.\\n- **exp_qwen25_14b_fewnerd** (success): The correlation-selection gap pattern is consistent at 14B scale, filling the gap between 8B and 32B. Qwen2.5-14B QLoRA fine-tuned on FewNERD should show intermediate degeneracy rates and similar signal ranking patterns, providing a smooth scaling curve for the diagnostic findings.\\n- **exp_b1_scd_conll_control** (negative): Negative control: SCD on CoNLL where construction gain is negative (-0.4pp, high degeneracy 70%). If SCD learns from construction pseudo-labels, it should NOT improve greedy F1 on a dataset where construction itself hurts. Expected: no improvement or slight degradation. Validates SCD mechanism.\\n\\n## 趋势\\n成功 103 / 负向 37 / 失败 4 / 共 173\\n\\n## 下一步\\n（无活跃 idea，请通过 ideas.md 添加）\\n\\n## 资源状态\\nautodl-5090: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\\nautodl-5090-b: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\\nautodl-5090-c: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\\n\\n## 告警\\n- ft_002: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- mvp_pilot_002: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp_007_ner_llama: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp001_seed123: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp001_seed456: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp008_re_n16: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_002_merge_conll2003: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_001_n16_seed123_full: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_001_n16_seed456_full: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_008_re_n16_full: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_001_seed42_v2_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_001_seed123_v2_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_001_seed456_v2_ner_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_002_conll_n16_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_007_llama_n16_r1024: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_018_llama_scierc_seed456: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_019_supervised_verifier: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_lp_t07_scierc: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_freeform_seed789_v2: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune_v2: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_finetune_v3: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp_qwen3_4b_conll_merge_inference_v2: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_qwen3_4b_scierc_merge_inference: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_tacred_re_benchmark: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_vc_lp_cascade_selection: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- hidden_state_probe_conll_v2: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- hidden_state_probe_conll_full: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- dgs_pilot: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- mbr_pilot: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_031_fewnerd_t12: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_030_conll_t12: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp_14b_inference_scierc: 已被终止 — unknown\\n\", \"total_chars\": 7267, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"

  "task_prompt": "## 任务：探查 autodl-5090-c 上 ESJ 实验所需的环境\n\n**背景**：exp_esj_entity_self_judgment 需要在 autodl-5090-c cuda:1 上运行。之前 Worker 错误地连到了 autodl-5090（另一台机器）。现在需要检查 autodl-5090-c 上的环境。\n\n**注意：连接的是 autodl-5090-c，不是 autodl-5090！**\n\n### 步骤\n\n1. SSH 连接到 **autodl-5090-c**（用 ssh_read_only），执行以下探查：\n\n```bash\n# 1. 检查 run_esj.py 是否存在\nfind /root -name \"run_esj.py\" -type f 2>/dev/null\n\n# 2. 检查项目代码目录\nls -la /root/struct_self_consist_ie/ 2>/dev/null || echo \"NO_PROJECT_DIR\"\nls -la /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/ 2>/dev/null || echo \"NO_PROJECT_DIR_TMP\"\n\n# 3. 检查 FewNERD 数据\nfind /root -path \"*fewnerd*\" -o -path \"*FewNERD*\" -o -path \"*few_nerd*\" 2>/dev/null | head -20\nfind /root -path \"*few-nerd*\" 2>/dev/null | head -10\n\n# 4. 检查可用模型（所有 Qwen 相关的模型目录）\nfind /root -maxdepth 4 -type d -name \"*Qwen*\" -o -name \"*qwen*\" 2>/dev/null | head -20\nfind /root/autodl-tmp -maxdepth 4 -type d -name \"*Qwen*\" -o -name \"*qwen*\" 2>/dev/null | head -20\nls -la /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/hub/ 2>/dev/null | head -30\nls -la /root/autodl-tmp/models/ 2>/dev/null | head -20\n\n# 5. GPU 状态确认\nnvidia-smi --query-gpu=index,name,memory.used,memory.total,utilization.gpu --format=csv,noheader\n\n# 6. Python/torch 环境\nwhich python3 && python3 --version\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh 2>/dev/null && conda activate base 2>/dev/null && which python && python --version\npip list 2>/dev/null | grep -iE \"torch|transformers|vllm|flash\" || echo \"NO_ML_PKGS\"\n\n# 7. 磁盘空间\ndf -h /root /root/autodl-tmp 2>/dev/null\n```\n\n2. **汇报以下信息**（必须明确回答每一项）：\n   - run_esj.py 是否存在？在哪个路径？\n   - FewNERD 数据是否存在？在哪个路径？\n   - 有哪些 Qwen 模型可用？列出所有找到的模型目录及大小\n   - cuda:1 是否空闲？\n   - Python/torch 环境是否就绪？\n   - 磁盘空间是否充足？\n\n**重要：只做读取探查，不要安装任何东西、不要创建文件、不要执行任何修改操作。**",

**Status:** QUEUED (as of 2026-05-20 16:21:52)

**Hypothesis:** "Multi-Round Iterative SC: N inverse scaling reveals disputed entities cause failures. Focused re-prompting on disputed entities (agreement < 0.6) with weighted aggregation (Round 2 weight=2) should reduce error rate on disputed entities without affecting non-disputed ones, closing part of the selection gap through iterative refinement rather than single-round aggregation."

- exp_qwen25_14b_fewnerd: Qwen2.5-14B fine-tuned, data at same path

- Multiple Qwen3-8B FewNERD experiments with N=8 samples

For MRSC to work, I need the existing N-sample inference outputs from FewNERD to identify disputed entities, then re-prompt the model for Round 2. Since both MRSC and ESJ are on autodl-5090-c and the Director said not to self-select, I should wait for the next step.

But I committed to pushing forward at full speed, so I'll spawn a Worker to write the MRSC code locally while waiting for the server investigation. The code needs to load the inference samples, compute agreement per entity, identify disputed ones, generate Round 2 prompts, run inference, aggregate with weighted voting, and evaluate—but first I need to understand what format the inference outputs are in.

Actually, spawning another Worker to check the same server would be redundant since ESJ Worker is already doing that. Better to have one Worker write the MRSC code locally by examining existing patterns like run_esj.py, then once ESJ Worker returns with the server state and available models, I can deploy both. <details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01BmHZpf5q9wSoPCYPzmohwJ`</summary>

```text
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Entity-Level Self-Judgment (ESJ) for structured self-consistency IE.
3	
4	For each unique entity across N=8 samples, uses the FT model to do a
5	yes/no verification forward pass. P(yes) serves as entity-level confidence
6	for weighted construction.
7	"""
8	
9	import argparse
10	import json
11	import os
12	import sys
13	import time
14	from collections import defaultdict
15	
16	import numpy as np
17	import torch
18	from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM
19	from peft import PeftModel
20	from scipy.stats import spearmanr
21	from sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score
22	
23	
24	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
25	# Data
26	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
27	
28	def load_samples(path, max_instances=0):
29	    data = []
30	    with open(path) as f:
31	        for line in f:
32	            if line.strip():
33	                data.append(json.loads(line))
34	                if 0 < max_instances <= len(data):
35	                    break
36	    return data
37	
38	
39	def extract_unique_entities(instance):
40	    """Return {(start, end, type, text): count_across_samples}."""
41	    emap = defaultdict(int)
42	    for sample in instance["samples"]:
43	        for e in sample.get("entities", []):
44	            emap[(e["start"], e["end"], e["type"], e["text"])] += 1
45	    return emap
46	
47	
48	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
49	# Prompt
50	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
51	
52	def build_verification_prompt(sentence, entity_text, entity_type):
53	    return (
54	        f'In the following sentence, is "{entity_text}" a {entity_type} entity? '
55	        f'Answer only yes or no.\n\n'
56	        f'Sentence: {sentence}\n'
57	        f'Answer:'
58	    )
59	
60	
61	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
62	# Model
63	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
64	
65	def load_model(base_path, adapter_path, device="cuda"):
66	    tok_path = adapter_path if adapter_path else base_path
67	    print(f"Loading tokenizer from {tok_path} ...")
68	    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
69	        tok_path, trust_remote_code=True, padding_side="left",
70	    )
71	    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:
72	        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token
73	
74	    print(f"Loading base model from {base_path} ...")
75	    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
76	        base_path,
77	        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,
78	        device_map=device,
79	        trust_remote_code=True,
80	    )
81	
82	    if adapter_path:
83	        print(f"Loading LoRA adapter from {adapter_path} ...")
84	        model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, adapter_path)
85	        model = model.merge_and_unload()
86	        print("LoRA merged.")
87	
88	    model.eval()
89	    return model, tokenizer
90	
91	
92	def get_yes_no_ids(tokenizer):
93	    yes_ids, no_ids = [], []
94	    for w in ["yes", "Yes", "YES"]:
95	        ids = tokenizer.encode(w, add_special_tokens=False)
96	        if len(ids) == 1:
97	            yes_ids.append(ids[0])
98	    for w in ["no", "No", "NO"]:
99	        ids = tokenizer.encode(w, add_special_tokens=False)
100	        if len(ids) == 1:
101	            no_ids.append(ids[0])
102	    yes_ids = list(set(yes_ids))
103	    no_ids = list(set(no_ids))
104	    print(f"yes token IDs: {yes_ids}  no token IDs: {no_ids}")
105	    assert yes_ids and no_ids, "Could not find yes/no token IDs"
106	    return yes_ids, no_ids
107	
108	
109	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
110	# Batch verification
111	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
112	
113	@torch.no_grad()
114	def batch_verify(model, tokenizer, prompts, yes_ids, no_ids,
115	                 batch_size=16, device="cuda"):
116	    yes_t = torch.tensor(yes_ids, device=device)
117	    no_t = torch.tensor(no_ids, device=device)
118	    all_p_yes = []
119	
120	    n_batches = (len(prompts) + batch_size - 1) // batch_size
121	    for bi in range(n_batches):
122	        start = bi * batch_size
123	        batch = prompts[start:start + batch_size]
124	
125	        formatted = []
126	        for p in batch:
127	            msgs = [{"role": "user", "content": p}]
128	            try:
129	                fp = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(
130	                    msgs, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True,
131	                    enable_thinking=False,
132	                )
133	            except TypeError:
134	                fp = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(
135	                    msgs, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True,
136	                )
137	            formatted.append(fp)
138	
139	        inputs = tokenizer(
140	            formatted, return_tensors="pt", padding=True,
141	            truncation=True, max_length=512,
142	        ).to(device)
143	
144	        logits = model(**inputs).logits  # (B, L, V)
145	
146	        for i in range(len(batch)):
147	            last_pos = inputs.attention_mask[i].sum() - 1
148	            next_logits = logits[i, last_pos]
149	            y_logits = next_logits[yes_t]
150	            n_logits = next_logits[no_t]
151	            combined = torch.cat([y_logits, n_logits])
152	            probs = torch.softmax(combined, dim=0)
153	            p_yes = probs[:len(yes_ids)].sum().item()
154	            all_p_yes.append(p_yes)
155	
156	        if bi % 100 == 0:
157	            print(f"  batch {bi}/{n_batches}  avg_p_yes={np.mean(all_p_yes[-len(batch):]):.3f}")
158	
159	    return all_p_yes
160	
161	
162	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
163	# Eval helpers
164	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
165	
166	def entity_set(entities):
167	    return {(e["start"], e["end"], e["type"]) for e in entities}
168	
169	
170	def compute_prf(pred, gold):
171	    if not gold and not pred:
172	        return 1.0, 1.0, 1.0
173	    if not pred or not gold:
174	        return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0
175	    tp = len(pred & gold)
176	    if tp == 0:
177	        return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0
178	    p = tp / len(pred)
179	    r = tp / len(gold)
180	    return p, r, 2 * p * r / (p + r)
181	
182	
183	def majority_vote(instance, threshold=0.5):
184	    counts = defaultdict(int)
185	    N = len(instance["samples"])
186	    for s in instance["samples"]:
187	        for e in s.get("entities", []):
188	            counts[(e["start"], e["end"], e["type"])] += 1
189	    return {k for k, c in counts.items() if c / N >= threshold}
190	
191	
192	def esj_construction(instance, esj_scores, threshold):
193	    counts = defaultdict(int)
194	    N = len(instance["samples"])
195	    for s in instance["samples"]:
196	        for e in s.get("entities", []):
197	            counts[(e["start"], e["end"], e["type"])] += 1
198	    out = set()
199	    for key, c in counts.items():
200	        p_yes = esj_scores.get(key, 0.0)
201	        if p_yes * (c / N) >= threshold:
202	            out.add(key)
203	    return out
204	
205	
206	def safe_spearman(a, b):
207	    mask = np.isfinite(a) & np.isfinite(b)
208	    if mask.sum() < 3:
209	        return float("nan"), float("nan")
210	    return spearmanr(a[mask], b[mask])
211	
212	
213	def safe_auroc(scores, labels):
214	    mask = np.isfinite(scores)
215	    s, l = scores[mask], labels[mask]
216	    if len(set(l)) < 2:
217	        return float("nan")
218	    try:
219	        return roc_auc_score(l, s)
220	    except Exception:
221	        return float("nan")
222	
223	
224	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
225	# Within-instance sample ranking (ESJ can rank samples, LP cannot for entity-level)
226	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
227	
228	def compute_sample_esj_scores(instance, esj_scores):
229	    """Mean P(yes) across entities in each sample."""
230	    per_sample = []
231	    for s in instance["samples"]:
232	        ents = s.get("entities", [])
233	        if not ents:
234	            per_sample.append(0.0)
235	            continue
236	        pyes_vals = []
237	        for e in ents:
238	            key = (e["start"], e["end"], e["type"])
239	            pyes_vals.append(esj_scores.get(key, 0.0))
240	        per_sample.append(float(np.mean(pyes_vals)))
241	    return per_sample
242	
243	
244	def compute_sample_lp_scores(instance):
245	    out = []
246	    for s in instance["samples"]:
247	        lp = s.get("mean_logprob")
248	        if lp is None:
249	            lp = s.get("cumulative_logprob", -999) / max(s.get("n_tokens", 1), 1)
250	        out.append(lp)
251	    return out
252	
253	
254	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
255	# Main
256	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
257	
258	def main():
259	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
260	    ap.add_argument("--base_model_path", type=str, required=True)
261	    ap.add_argument("--adapter_path", type=str, default=None)
262	    ap.add_argument("--data_path", type=str, required=True)
263	    ap.add_argument("--output_dir", type=str, required=True)
264	    ap.add_argument("--max_instances", type=int, default=0)
265	    ap.add_argument("--batch_size", type=int, default=16)
266	    args = ap.parse_args()
267	
268	    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)
269	    device = "cuda"
270	
271	    # ---- 1. Load data ----
272	    print(f"Loading data from {args.data_path} ...")
273	    data = load_samples(args.data_path, args.max_instances)
274	    print(f"  {len(data)} instances loaded")
275	
276	    # ---- 2. Collect unique entities ----
277	    print("Extracting unique entities ...")
278	    inst_emaps = []
279	    total_occ, total_uniq = 0, 0
280	    for inst in data:
281	        em = extract_unique_entities(inst)
282	        inst_emaps.append(em)
283	        total_occ += sum(em.values())
284	        total_uniq += len(em)
285	    print(f"  {total_uniq} unique entities to verify ({total_occ} total occurrences)")
286	    print(f"  avg {total_uniq/len(data):.1f} unique / {total_occ/len(data):.1f} total per instance")
287	
288	    # ---- 3. Build prompts ----
289	    all_prompts = []
290	    prompt_idx = []  # (instance_i, (start, end, type))
291	    for i, inst in enumerate(data):
292	        for (s, e, t, txt), _ in inst_emaps[i].items():
293	            all_prompts.append(build_verification_prompt(inst["text"], txt, t))
294	            prompt_idx.append((i, (s, e, t)))
295	    print(f"  {len(all_prompts)} verification prompts built")
296	
297	    # ---- 4. Load model ----
298	    model, tokenizer = load_model(args.base_model_path, args.adapter_path, device)
299	    yes_ids, no_ids = get_yes_no_ids(tokenizer)
300	
301	    # ---- 5. Run verification ----
302	    print(f"Running batch verification (batch_size={args.batch_size}) ...")
303	    t0 = time.time()
304	    p_yes_all = batch_verify(model, tokenizer, all_prompts, yes_ids, no_ids,
305	                             args.batch_size, device)
306	    elapsed = time.time() - t0
307	    print(f"  Done in {elapsed:.1f}s ({len(all_prompts)/elapsed:.0f} prompts/s)")
308	
309	    del model
310	    import gc; gc.collect(); torch.cuda.empty_cache()
311	
312	    # ---- 6. Map P(yes) back ----
313	    inst_esj = [dict() for _ in range(len(data))]
314	    for idx, (ii, key) in enumerate(prompt_idx):
315	        inst_esj[ii][key] = p_yes_all[idx]
316	
317	    # ---- 7. Baselines ----
318	    print("Computing baselines ...")
319	    greedy_f1s, oracle_f1s = [], []
320	    for inst in data:
321	        gold = entity_set(inst["gold"]["entities"])
322	        g = entity_set(inst.get("greedy", inst["samples"][0]).get("entities", []))
323	        greedy_f1s.append(compute_prf(g, gold)[2])
324	        oracle_f1s.append(max(
325	            compute_prf(entity_set(s.get("entities", [])), gold)[2]
326	            for s in inst["samples"]
327	        ))
328	    greedy_f1 = float(np.mean(greedy_f1s))
329	    oracle_f1 = float(np.mean(oracle_f1s))
330	
331	    mv_ths = [0.125, 0.25, 0.375, 0.5, 0.625, 0.75]
332	    mv_res = {}
333	    for th in mv_ths:
334	        fs = []
335	        for i, inst in enumerate(data):
336	            gold = entity_set(inst["gold"]["entities"])
337	            fs.append(compute_prf(majority_vote(inst, th), gold)[2])
338	        mv_res[str(th)] = float(np.mean(fs))
339	    best_mv_th = max(mv_res, key=mv_res.get)
340	    best_mv_f1 = mv_res[best_mv_th]
341	
342	    # ---- 8. ESJ construction ----
343	    print("Evaluating ESJ construction ...")
344	    esj_ths = [0.02, 0.05, 0.08, 0.1, 0.12, 0.15, 0.2, 0.25, 0.3, 0.35, 0.4, 0.45, 0.5]
345	    esj_res = {}
346	    for th in esj_ths:
347	        fs = []
348	        for i, inst in enumerate(data):
349	            gold = entity_set(inst["gold"]["entities"])
350	            fs.append(compute_prf(esj_construction(inst, inst_esj[i], th), gold)[2])
351	        esj_res[str(th)] = float(np.mean(fs))
352	    best_esj_th = max(esj_res, key=esj_res.get)
353	    best_esj_f1 = esj_res[best_esj_th]
354

      "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Entity-Level Self-Judgment (ESJ) for structured self-consistency IE.\n\nFor each unique entity across N=8 samples, uses the FT model to do a\nyes/no verification forward pass. P(yes) serves as entity-level confidence\nfor weighted construction.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nfrom peft import PeftModel\nfrom scipy.stats import spearmanr\nfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Data\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef load_samples(path, max_instances=0):\n    data = []\n    with open(path) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            if line.strip():\n                data.append(json.loads(line))\n                if 0 < max_instances <= len(data):\n                    break\n    return data\n\n\ndef extract_unique_entities(instance):\n    \"\"\"Return {(start, end, type, text): count_across_samples}.\"\"\"\n    emap = defaultdict(int)\n    for sample in instance[\"samples\"]:\n        for e in sample.get(\"entities\", []):\n            emap[(e[\"start\"], e[\"end\"], e[\"type\"], e[\"text\"])] += 1\n    return emap\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Prompt\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef build_verification_prompt(sentence, entity_text, entity_type):\n    return (\n        f'In the following sentence, is \"{entity_text}\" a {entity_type} entity? '\n        f'Answer only yes or no.\\n\\n'\n        f'Sentence: {sentence}\\n'\n        f'Answer:'\n    )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Model\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef load_model(base_path, adapter_path, device=\"cuda\"):\n    tok_path = adapter_path if adapter_path else base_path\n    print(f\"Loading tokenizer from {tok_path} ...\")\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\n        tok_path, trust_remote_code=True, padding_side=\"left\",\n    )\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\n\n    print(f\"Loading base model from {base_path} ...\")\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n        base_path,\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\n        device_map=device,\n        trust_remote_code=True,\n    )\n\n    if adapter_path:\n        print(f\"Loading LoRA adapter from {adapter_path} ...\")\n        model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, adapter_path)\n        model = model.merge_and_unload()\n        print(\"LoRA merged.\")\n\n    model.eval()\n    return model, tokenizer\n\n\ndef get_yes_no_ids(tokenizer):\n    yes_ids, no_ids = [], []\n    for w in [\"yes\", \"Yes\", \"YES\"]:\n        ids = tokenizer.encode(w, add_special_tokens=False)\n        if len(ids) == 1:\n            yes_ids.append(ids[0])\n    for w in [\"no\", \"No\", \"NO\"]:\n        ids = tokenizer.encode(w, add_special_tokens=False)\n        if len(ids) == 1:\n            no_ids.append(ids[0])\n    yes_ids = list(set(yes_ids))\n    no_ids = list(set(no_ids))\n    print(f\"yes token IDs: {yes_ids}  no token IDs: {no_ids}\")\n    assert yes_ids and no_ids, \"Could not find yes/no token IDs\"\n    return yes_ids, no_ids\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Batch verification\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef batch_verify(model, tokenizer, prompts, yes_ids, no_ids,\n                 batch_size=16, device=\"cuda\"):\n    yes_t = torch.tensor(yes_ids, device=device)\n    no_t = torch.tensor(no_ids, device=device)\n    all_p_yes = []\n\n    n_batches = (len(prompts) + batch_size - 1) // batch_size\n    for bi in range(n_batches):\n        start = bi * batch_size\n        batch = prompts[start:start + batch_size]\n\n        formatted = []\n        for p in batch:\n            msgs = [{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": p}]\n            try:\n                fp = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\n                    msgs, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True,\n                    enable_thinking=False,\n                )\n            except TypeError:\n                fp = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\n                    msgs, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True,\n                )\n            formatted.append(fp)\n\n        inputs = tokenizer(\n            formatted, return_tensors=\"pt\", padding=True,\n            truncation=True, max_length=512,\n        ).to(device)\n\n        logits = model(**inputs).logits  # (B, L, V)\n\n        for i in range(len(batch)):\n            last_pos = inputs.attention_mask[i].sum() - 1\n            next_logits = logits[i, last_pos]\n            y_logits = next_logits[yes_t]\n            n_logits = next_logits[no_t]\n            combined = torch.cat([y_logits, n_logits])\n            probs = torch.softmax(combined, dim=0)\n            p_yes = probs[:len(yes_ids)].sum().item()\n            all_p_yes.append(p_yes)\n\n        if bi % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"  batch {bi}/{n_batches}  avg_p_yes={np.mean(all_p_yes[-len(batch):]):.3f}\")\n\n    return all_p_yes\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Eval helpers\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef entity_set(entities):\n    return {(e[\"start\"], e[\"end\"], e[\"type\"]) for e in entities}\n\n\ndef compute_prf(pred, gold):\n    if not gold and not pred:\n        return 1.0, 1.0, 1.0\n    if not pred or not gold:\n        return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0\n    tp = len(pred & gold)\n    if tp == 0:\n        return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0\n    p = tp / len(pred)\n    r = tp / len(gold)\n    return p, r, 2 * p * r / (p + r)\n\n\ndef majority_vote(instance, threshold=0.5):\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    N = len(instance[\"samples\"])\n    for s in instance[\"samples\"]:\n        for e in s.get(\"entities\", []):\n            counts[(e[\"start\"], e[\"end\"], e[\"type\"])] += 1\n    return {k for k, c in counts.items() if c / N >= threshold}\n\n\ndef esj_construction(instance, esj_scores, threshold):\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    N = len(instance[\"samples\"])\n    for s in instance[\"samples\"]:\n        for e in s.get(\"entities\", []):\n            counts[(e[\"start\"], e[\"end\"], e[\"type\"])] += 1\n    out = set()\n    for key, c in counts.items():\n        p_yes = esj_scores.get(key, 0.0)\n        if p_yes * (c / N) >= threshold:\n            out.add(key)\n    return out\n\n\ndef safe_spearman(a, b):\n    mask = np.isfinite(a) & np.isfinite(b)\n    if mask.sum() < 3:\n        return float(\"nan\"), float(\"nan\")\n    return spearmanr(a[mask], b[mask])\n\n\ndef safe_auroc(scores, labels):\n    mask = np.isfinite(scores)\n    s, l = scores[mask], labels[mask]\n    if len(set(l)) < 2:\n        return float(\"nan\")\n    try:\n        return roc_auc_score(l, s)\n    except Exception:\n        return float(\"nan\")\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Within-instance sample ranking (ESJ can rank samples, LP cannot for entity-level)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef compute_sample_esj_scores(instance, esj_scores):\n    \"\"\"Mean P(yes) across entities in each sample.\"\"\"\n    per_sample = []\n    for s in instance[\"samples\"]:\n        ents = s.get(\"entities\", [])\n        if not ents:\n            per_sample.append(0.0)\n            continue\n        pyes_vals = []\n        for e in ents:\n            key = (e[\"start\"], e[\"end\"], e[\"type\"])\n            pyes_vals.append(esj_scores.get(key, 0.0))\n        per_sample.append(float(np.mean(pyes_vals)))\n    return per_sample\n\n\ndef compute_sample_lp_scores(instance):\n    out = []\n    for s in instance[\"samples\"]:\n        lp = s.get(\"mean_logprob\")\n        if lp is None:\n            lp = s.get(\"cumulative_logprob\", -999) / max(s.get(\"n_tokens\", 1), 1)\n        out.append(lp)\n    return out\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Main\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    ap.add_argument(\"--base_model_path\", type=str, required=True)\n    ap.add_argument(\"--adapter_path\", type=str, default=None)\n    ap.add_argument(\"--data_path\", type=str, required=True)\n    ap.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", type=str, required=True)\n    ap.add_argument(\"--max_instances\", type=int, default=0)\n    ap.add_argument(\"--batch_size\", type=int, default=16)\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    device = \"cuda\"\n\n    # ---- 1. Load data ----\n    print(f\"Loading data from {args.data_path} ...\")\n    data = load_samples(args.data_path, args.max_instances)\n    print(f\"  {len(data)} instances loaded\")\n\n    # ---- 2. Collect unique entities ----\n    print(\"Extracting unique entities ...\")\n    inst_emaps = []\n    total_occ, total_uniq = 0, 0\n    for inst in data:\n        em = extract_unique_entities(inst)\n        inst_emaps.append(em)\n        total_occ += sum(em.values())\n        total_uniq += len(em)\n    print(f\"  {total_uniq} unique entities to verify ({total_occ} total occurrences)\")\n    print(f\"  avg {total_uniq/len(data):.1f} unique / {total_occ/len(data):.1f} total per instance\")\n\n    # ---- 3. Build prompts ----\n    all_prompts = []\n    prompt_idx = []  # (instance_i, (start, end, type))\n    for i, inst in enumerate(data):\n        for (s, e, t, txt), _ in inst_emaps[i].items():\n            all_prompts.append(build_verification_prompt(inst[\"text\"], txt, t))\n            prompt_idx.append((i, (s, e, t)))\n    print(f\"  {len(all_prompts)} verification prompts built\")\n\n    # ---- 4. Load model ----\n    model, tokenizer = load_model(args.base_model_path, args.adapter_path, device)\n    yes_ids, no_ids = get_yes_no_ids(tokenizer)\n\n    # ---- 5. Run verification ----\n    print(f\"Running batch verification (batch_size={args.batch_size}) ...\")\n    t0 = time.time()\n    p_yes_all = batch_verify(model, tokenizer, all_prompts, yes_ids, no_ids,\n                             args.batch_size, device)\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"  Done in {elapsed:.1f}s ({len(all_prompts)/elapsed:.0f} prompts/s)\")\n\n    del model\n    import gc; gc.collect(); torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    # ---- 6. Map P(yes) back ----\n    inst_esj = [dict() for _ in range(len(data))]\n    for idx, (ii, key) in enumerate(prompt_idx):\n        inst_esj[ii][key] = p_yes_all[idx]\n\n    # ---- 7. Baselines ----\n    print(\"Computing baselines ...\")\n    greedy_f1s, oracle_f1s = [], []\n    for inst in data:\n        gold = entity_set(inst[\"gold\"][\"entities\"])\n        g = entity_set(inst.get(\"greedy\", inst[\"samples\"][0]).get(\"entities\", []))\n        greedy_f1s.append(compute_prf(g, gold)[2])\n        oracle_f1s.append(max(\n            compute_prf(entity_set(s.get(\"entities\", [])), gold)[2]\n            for s in inst[\"samples\"]\n        ))\n    greedy_f1 = float(np.mean(greedy_f1s))\n    oracle_f1 = float(np.mean(oracle_f1s))\n\n    mv_ths = [0.125, 0.25, 0.375, 0.5, 0.625, 0.75]\n    mv_res = {}\n    for th in mv_ths:\n        fs = []\n        for i, inst in enumerate(data):\n            gold = entity_set(inst[\"gold\"][\"entities\"])\n            fs.append(compute_prf(majority_vote(inst, th), gold)[2])\n        mv_res[str(th)] = float(np.mean(fs))\n    best_mv_th = max(mv_res, key=mv_res.get)\n    best_mv_f1 = mv_res[best_mv_th]\n\n    # ---- 8. ESJ construction ----\n    print(\"Evaluating ESJ construction ...\")\n    esj_ths = [0.02, 0.05, 0.08, 0.1, 0.12, 0.15, 0.2, 0.25, 0.3, 0.35, 0.4, 0.45, 0.5]\n    esj_res = {}\n    for th in esj_ths:\n        fs = []\n        for i, inst in enumerate(data):\n            gold = entity_set(inst[\"gold\"][\"entities\"])\n            fs.append(compute_prf(esj_construction(inst, inst_esj[i], th), gold)[2])\n        esj_res[str(th)] = float(np.mean(fs))\n    best_esj_th = max(esj_res, key=esj_res.get)\n    best_esj_f1 = esj_res[best_esj_th]\n\n    # ---- 9. Entity-level signal quality ----\n    print(\"Entity-level analysis ...\")\n    ent_correct, ent_esj, ent_agree = [], [], []\n    for i, inst in enumerate(data):\n        gold = entity_set(inst[\"gold\"][\"entities\"])\n        N = len(inst[\"samples\"])\n        counts = defaultdict(int)\n        for s in inst[\"samples\"]:\n            for e in s.get(\"entities\", []):\n                counts[(e[\"start\"], e[\"end\"], e[\"type\"])] += 1\n        for key, c in counts.items():\n            ent_correct.append(1 if key in gold else 0)\n            ent_esj.append(inst_esj[i].get(key, 0.0))\n            ent_agree.append(c / N)\n\n    ent_correct = np.array(ent_correct)\n    ent_esj_arr = np.array(ent_esj)\n    ent_agree_arr = np.array(ent_agree)\n    ent_combined = ent_esj_arr * ent_agree_arr\n\n    rho_esj, p_rho_esj = safe_spearman(ent_esj_arr, ent_correct.astype(float))\n    rho_agree, _ = safe_spearman(ent_agree_arr, ent_correct.astype(float))\n    rho_comb, _ = safe_spearman(ent_combined, ent_correct.astype(float))\n    auroc_esj = safe_auroc(ent_esj_arr, ent_correct)\n    auroc_agree = safe_auroc(ent_agree_arr, ent_correct)\n    auroc_comb = safe_auroc(ent_combined, ent_correct)\n\n    # ---- 10. Breakdown by agreement ----\n    disputed = ent_agree_arr < 0.5\n    consensus = ent_agree_arr >= 0.75\n    mid = (~disputed) & (~consensus)\n\n    def group_stats(mask, label):\n        n = int(mask.sum())\n        if n < 3:\n            return {\"n\": n}\n        return {\n            \"n\": n,\n            \"correct_rate\": round(float(ent_correct[mask].mean()), 4),\n            \"esj_mean\": round(float(ent_esj_arr[mask].mean()), 4),\n            \"esj_std\": round(float(ent_esj_arr[mask].std()), 4),\n            \"rho_esj_correct\": round(float(safe_spearman(ent_esj_arr[mask], ent_correct[mask].astype(float))[0]), 4),\n            \"auroc_esj\": round(safe_auroc(ent_esj_arr[mask], ent_correct[mask]), 4),\n        }\n\n    # ---- 11. Within-instance sample ranking ----\n    print(\"Within-instance sample ranking ...\")\n    esj_rank_corrs, lp_rank_corrs = [], []\n    for i, inst in enumerate(data):\n        gold = entity_set(inst[\"gold\"][\"entities\"])\n        sample_f1s = [compute_prf(entity_set(s.get(\"entities\", [])), gold)[2]\n                      for s in inst[\"samples\"]]\n        if len(set(sample_f1s)) < 2:\n            continue\n        esj_sample = compute_sample_esj_scores(inst, inst_esj[i])\n        lp_sample = compute_sample_lp_scores(inst)\n        r_esj, _ = spearmanr(esj_sample, sample_f1s)\n        r_lp, _ = spearmanr(lp_sample, sample_f1s)\n        if np.isfinite(r_esj):\n            esj_rank_corrs.append(r_esj)\n        if np.isfinite(r_lp):\n            lp_rank_corrs.append(r_lp)\n\n    # ESJ-based selection F1 (pick best sample by ESJ score)\n    esj_sel_f1s, lp_sel_f1s = [], []\n    for i, inst in enumerate(data):\n        gold = entity_set(inst[\"gold\"][\"entities\"])\n        sample_f1s = [compute_prf(entity_set(s.get(\"entities\", [])), gold)[2]\n                      for s in inst[\"samples\"]]\n        esj_sample = compute_sample_esj_scores(inst, inst_esj[i])\n        lp_sample = compute_sample_lp_scores(inst)\n        esj_sel_f1s.append(sample_f1s[int(np.argmax(esj_sample))])\n        lp_sel_f1s.append(sample_f1s[int(np.argmax(lp_sample))])\n\n    # ---- 12. P(yes) distribution ----\n    pya = np.array(p_yes_all)\n    p_yes_dist = {\n        \"mean\": round(float(pya.mean()), 4),\n        \"std\": round(float(pya.std()), 4),\n        \"median\": round(float(np.median(pya)), 4),\n        \"q25\": round(float(np.percentile(pya, 25)), 4),\n        \"q75\": round(float(np.percentile(pya, 75)), 4),\n    }\n\n    # ---- Build result ----\n    result = {\n        \"experiment\": \"ESJ_entity_self_judgment\",\n        \"model_base\": args.base_model_path,\n        \"adapter\": args.adapter_path,\n        \"data\": args.data_path,\n        \"n_instances\": len(data),\n        \"n_unique_entities\": total_uniq,\n        \"n_entity_occurrences\": total_occ,\n        \"avg_unique_per_instance\": round(total_uniq / len(data), 2),\n        \"verification_seconds\": round(elapsed, 1),\n        \"prompts_per_second\": round(len(all_prompts) / elapsed, 1),\n        \"p_yes_distribution\": p_yes_dist,\n        \"construction_f1\": {\n {\"threshold\": best_mv_th, \"f1\": round(best_mv_f1, 4)},\n            \"esj_weighted\": {k: round(v, 4) for k, v in esj_res.items()},\n            \"best_esj\": {\"threshold\": best_esj_th, \"f1\":   \"mv_vs_greedy\": round(best_mv_f1 - greedy_f1, 4),\n        },\n        \"entity_signal_quality\": {\n            \"n_entities\": len(ent_correct),\n            \"overall_correct_rate\": \"auroc\": round(float(auroc_agree), 4)},\n            \"esj_x_agreement\": {\"rho\": round(float(rho_comb), 4), \"auroc\": round(float(auroc_comb), 4)},\n        },\n        \"agreement_breakdown\": MRSC (Multi-Round Iterative SC):

4. Aggregate: Weight Round 2 results with weight=2, combine with Round 1

````json
{
  "task_type": "code:lsc_write_and_prepare",
  "project": "struct_self_consist_ie",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：编写 LSC（Latent Self-Consistency）实验脚本\n\n### 背景\n\nexp_lsc_latent_sc 已审批通过（QUEUED），将在 autodl-5090-c cuda:2 上运行。\n\n**Hypothesis**: Latent Self-Consistency: F4 diagnostic proves LP is compressed (median range 0.041 nats). Hidden states contain richer per-sample information than scalar LP. Entity-level mean-pooled hidden state representations enable latent aggregation (centroid selection, latent confidence scoring, clustering) that bypasses LP compression and provides discriminative per-sample ranking unavailable from output-space signals.\n\n**Parent**: exp_e2_mixture_of_signals（F4 LP-only diagnostic 实验，证明 LP 信息被压缩）\n\n### 算法设计\n\n核心思路：LP 是 scalar summary，信息量有限。直接用 hidden states 做 entity 表示，应该能提供更丰富的 per-sample 信号。\n\n**Pipeline**:\n1. **加载数据**：现有 N=8 inference samples（JSONL，与 ESJ 同格式：每行一个 instance，有 `text`, `samples`, `gold`, `greedy`）\n2. **Forward pass 提取 hidden states**：对每个 sample 的 output（即模型生成的 JSON NER 结果），做 forward pass，提取最后一层 hidden states。\n   - 输入 = chat template 格式的完整 prompt + generated output\n   - 提取 generated output 部分的 token-level hidden states\n3. **Entity span 定位**：在 generated output 的 token 序列中，找到每个 entity text span 对应的 token 位置\n   - 需要 tokenize generated output，然后用 char_to_token mapping 找到 entity text 的 token range\n4. **Entity-level mean pooling**：对每个 entity 的 token range 做 mean pooling，得到 entity hidden state vector\n5. **三种 Latent Aggregation 方法**：\n   \n   **A) Centroid Selection**: 对每个 instance 的所有 samples 的 entity sets，计算每个 sample 的 \"latent centroid\"（所有 entity vectors 的均值）。选与 centroid 最近的 sample（cosine similarity）。\n   \n   **B) Latent Confidence Scoring**: 对每个 entity，收集它在不同 samples 中的 hidden state vectors，计算 cosine similarity 的均值（intra-entity agreement in latent space）。高 latent agreement = 模型对这个 entity 更确定。用这个分数做 entity-level weighted construction。\n   \n   **C) Clustering**: 对每个 instance，在 entity hidden state space 做 k-means（k = 设为 2 或 auto），看 cluster 分布是否能区分 correct/incorrect entities。\n   \n6. **Baselines**: greedy, majority vote (various θ), LP-based selection, oracle\n7. **评估**: F1, selection F1, entity-level ρ/AUROC\n\n### 技术挑战与解决方案\n\n**Entity span 定位问题**：生成的 JSON 中 entity text 的 token 位置需要精确对齐。\n- 方案：tokenize 完整 generated output，然后用 string matching 找 entity text 的 char offset，再用 tokenizer 的 offset_mapping 转为 token indices\n- Fallback：如果精确对齐失败，用 entity text 单独 tokenize + 在 hidden states 序列中滑动窗口匹配\n\n**显存管理**：需要存储 hidden states（每个 token ~4096 维 for 7B model），N=8 samples × ~500 instances = ~4000 forward passes。\n- 方案：逐 sample 处理，不缓存所有 hidden states，处理完一个 instance 的所有 samples 后立即聚合并释放\n\n### 脚本参数\n\n```\n--base_model_path   # 基础模型路径\n--adapter_path      # LoRA adapter（可选）\n--data_path         # inference samples JSONL\n--output_dir        # 输出目录\n--hidden_layer      # 取哪一层 hidden state，默认 -1（最后一层）\n--batch_size        # forward pass batch size，默认 4（比 ESJ 小，因为需要存 hidden states）\n--max_instances     # 调试用，默认 0\n```\n\n### 参考代码\n\n读取 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/experiments/esj/run_esj.py` 了解：\n- 数据加载格式（load_samples, extract_unique_entities）\n- 模型加载模式（load_model with base + adapter）\n- 评估函数（compute_prf, majority_vote, entity_set）\n- 输出格式\n\nLSC 需要复用这些基础函数，但核心逻辑完全不同（hidden state extraction + latent aggregation vs yes/no verification）。\n\n### 产出\n\n1. 写 `run_lsc.py` 到 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/experiments/lsc/run_lsc.py`\n2. **代码质量**：文件顶部注释、关键函数 docstring、特殊逻辑注释\n3. 不需要 SSH——只写本地代码\n4. 写完后确认代码语法正确（`python -c \"import ast; ast.parse(open('path').read())\"` 验证）",
  "repo_path": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie"
}

````json
{
  "task_type": "code:asb_write",
  "project": "struct_self_consist_ie",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：编写 ASB（Adaptive Sample Budget）实验脚本\n\n### 背景\n\nexp_asb_adaptive_budget 已审批通过（QUEUED）。\n\n**Hypothesis**: Adaptive Sample Budget: extension of E_θ threshold optimization to sample count N. Instance features (degeneracy, LP variance, agreement rate) predict optimal N per instance. Under fixed total budget=8 (average), adaptive allocation outperforms uniform N=8 by allocating more samples to high-uncertainty instances and fewer to degenerate/easy ones.\n\n### 核心思路\n\n现有 N=16 inference 数据可以 subsample 到 N=2,4,8。对每个 instance，不同 N 值的 construction F1 不同。如果我们能用 instance features（从 N=2 或 N=4 的小样本就能计算的 cheap features）预测\"这个 instance 需要多少 samples\"，就可以在固定总 budget（平均 N=8）下做 adaptive allocation：easy instances 用 N=2-4，hard instances 用 N=12-16。\n\n### 算法设计\n\n**Phase 1: 数据准备**（从现有 N=16 JSONL 做 subsampling）\n1. 加载 N=16 inference samples（FewNERD, SciERC, CoNLL 如果有）\n2. 对每个 instance，生成 N∈{2,4,8,16} 的 subsamples（10 random subsets per N，取平均）\n3. 对每个 (instance, N) 组合，计算：\n   - construction F1（majority vote at best θ）\n   - oracle F1\n   - degeneracy rate\n   - LP variance（within-sample LP range）\n   - agreement rate（entity-level average agreement）\n\n**Phase 2: Instance Features**（可从 N=2 cheap 计算的特征）\n- `degen_rate_n2`: N=2 时的 degeneracy rate（两个 sample 完全一致的比例）\n- `lp_variance_n2`: N=2 时的 LP 方差\n- `agreement_n2`: N=2 时的 entity agreement\n- `entity_count_n2`: N=2 时平均 entity 数量\n- `text_length`: 句子长度（char count）\n- `greedy_f1`: greedy baseline F1（如果有 gold，否则跳过此特征）\n\n**Phase 3: 最优 N 标签**\n- 对每个 instance，找使 F1 增益 ≥ 95% of max gain 的最小 N（节约 budget）\n- 标签 = optimal_N ∈ {2, 4, 8, 16}\n\n**Phase 4: Predictor 训练**\n- 用 Phase 2 的 features 预测 Phase 3 的 optimal_N\n- 模型：LogisticRegression (ordinal), RandomForest, MLP\n- 评估：5-fold CV，metric = macro F1 of N-class prediction\n\n**Phase 5: Budget-Constrained Evaluation**\n- 给定总 budget B = 8 * n_instances\n- Predictor 为每个 instance 分配 N_i\n- 如果 Σ N_i > B，按优先级裁剪（先砍 N_i 最大的）\n- 如果 Σ N_i < B，把余量分给最 uncertain 的 instances\n- 计算 adaptive 的 macro F1 vs uniform N=8 的 macro F1\n\n### 脚本参数\n\n```\n--data_paths       # N=16 inference JSONL paths（逗号分隔，支持多数据集）\n--dataset_names    # 对应的数据集名（逗号分隔）\n--output_dir       # 输出目录\n--n_subsamples     # 每个 N 的随机 subset 数，默认 10\n--budget_per_instance  # 平均 budget，默认 8\n--max_instances    # 调试用，默认 0\n--seed             # 随机种子，默认 42\n```\n\n### 关键：这是 CPU-only 实验\n\n不需要 GPU。不需要模型 forward pass。全部基于现有 N=16 数据做 subsampling 分析。\n\n### 参考代码\n\n读取 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/experiments/esj/run_esj.py` 了解数据格式（JSONL with `text`, `samples`, `gold`, `greedy` fields）和评估函数（compute_prf, majority_vote, entity_set）。\n\n也可参考 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/n_inverse_scaling/run_analysis.py` 如果存在，了解 subsampling 模式。\n\n### 产出\n\n1. 写 `run_asb.py` 到 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_1/projects/struct_self_consist_ie/artifacts/experiments/asb/run_asb.py`\n2. 代码质量：文件顶部注释、关键函数 docstring\n3. 确认语法正确\n4. 在输出的 summary 中明确标注：这是 CPU-only 实验，不需要 GPU",

}

2. MRSC 代码编写 Worker (code:mrsc_write_and_prepare)


3. LSC 代码编写 Worker (code:lsc_write_and_prepare)
4. ASB 代码编写 Worker (code:asb_write)

Director 说 GPU 空转数小时，需要立即执行。虽然 MRSC 和 LSC 的脚本可能还在编写中，但 Director 要求不等待，直接 spawn Worker 开始执行。

每个 Worker 需要在 autodl-5090-c 上检查环境、同步或编写脚本、执行实验，然后用 submit_training_job 提交。关于模型选择，Director 之前说过要先汇报可用模型，但现在"立即启动"的优先级更高，所以我需要在这个约束下推进。

对于 MRSC 和 LSC，Director 在 ESJ 指令中的模型限制只针对 ESJ 特定问题，这两个实验没有被限制。它们都需要现有的 FewNERD inference samples 作为输入数据，然后 MRSC 需要用模型做 Round 2 验证，LSC 需要用模型提取 hidden states。

我检查了 autodl-5090-c 上的可用资源，发现之前训练过的 Qwen2 14B 和 7B 的 FewNERD checkpoints 都在那里，所以两个实验都有模型可用。

不过 FewNERD 的推理样本是用 Qwen3-8B 生成的，而 MRSC Round 2 的 re-prompting 需要和 Round 1 保持一致，这就有点复杂了。我决定启动 Worker 让它们自己去探查资源并处理这个问题，给每个 Worker 足够的上下文让它们能自主决策，包括先探查 autodl-5090-c 上的资源和写相关代码。

由于 code Worker 可能还在写代码，我应该让执行 Worker 自己写，这样就不用依赖 code Worker 了。对于模型选择，Director 提到的 ESJ 模型问题是原计划用 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 但 Worker 改成了 Qwen3-8B，但 MRSC 和 LSC 的假设没有指定特定模型——这两个实验处理的是现有的 FewNERD 推理样本，需要用生成这些样本的原始模型来做后续处理。我在 Worker prompt 里明确说明要使用生成原始样本的模型。

关于 submit_training_job，这些实验不是传统训练而是推理加分析，但系统规范要求 Worker 提交训练必须用 submit_training_job。虽然 submit_training_job 通常用于长时间运行的任务，但 MRSC 和 LSC 主要是推理工作（约 1-2 小时），应该用 submit_training_job 来管理。Worker 需要 SSH 到 autodl-5090-c，检查环境，写或部署代码，然后用 submit_training_job 提交，这需要 SSH 和 registry 权限。

现在我要立即并行启动两个 Worker。

Focused re-prompting on disputed entities (agreement < 0.6) with weighted aggregation (Round 2 weight=2) should reduce error rate on disputed entities without affecting non-disputed ones, closing part of the selection gap through iterative refinement rather than single-round aggregation.\n\n### 你要做什么\n\n**Step 1: 探查 autodl-5090-c 环境**（ssh_read_only）\n\n```bash\n# 环境检测\nsource 2>/dev/null\n\n# GPU 状态\nnvidia-smi --query-gpu=index,memory.used,memory.total,utilization.gpu --format=csv,noheader\n\n# Python 环境\nwhich python && python --version\npython -c \"import torch; print(f'torch={torch.__version__}, cuda={torch.cuda.is_available()}')\"\npip list 2>/dev/null | grep -iE \"torch|transformers|peft|scipy|sklearn\"\n\n# 磁盘\ndf -h /root/autodl-tmp\n```\n\n**Step 2: 写 run_mrsc.py 到服务器上**\n\n在 `/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/code/` 目录下创建 `run_mrsc.py`。\n\n算法：\n1. **Round 1 分析**：加载现有 N=8 FewNERD inference samples（JSONL，每行 instance with `text`, `samples`[list, each with `entities`], `gold`, `greedy`）\n2. **识别 disputed entities**：每个 entity 在 N samples 中出现的频率。Agreement < 0.6 的 = disputed\n3. **Round 2 Re-prompting**：对 disputed entities，用模型做 yes/no verification（类似 ESJ）：\n   ```\n   In the sentence below, is \"{entity_text}\" a {entity_type} entity? \n   Multiple extraction attempts disagreed ({count}/{N} samples included it).\n   Answer only yes or no.\n   \n   Sentence: {text}\n   Answer:\n   ```\n   用 batch forward pass，提取 P(yes) = softmax over yes/no token logits\n4. **加权聚合**：\n   - Non-disputed (agreement >= 0.6): 直接按 majority vote\n   - Disputed: combined_score = (round1_agreement * 1 + round2_p_yes * 2) / 3。combined_score >= threshold → include\n5. **Sweep thresholds**: [0.1, 0.15, 0.2, 0.25, 0.3, 0.35, 0.4, 0.45, 0.5]\n6. **Baselines**: greedy F1, majority vote (sweep θ), oracle F1\n7. **输出**: mrsc_results.json + 打印 summary\n\n脚本参数：\n```\n--base_model_path    # 基础模型\n--adapter_path       # LoRA adapter (可选)\n--data_path          # inference samples JSONL  \n--output_dir         # 输出\n--dispute_threshold  # 默认 0.6\n--round2_weight      # 默认 2.0\n--batch_size         # 默认 16\n--max_instances      # 默认 0 (全部)\n```\n\n模型加载参考（与 ESJ 相同模式）：\n```python\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nfrom peft import PeftModel\nimport torch\n\ntokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(tok_path, trust_remote_code=True, padding_side=\"left\")\nmodel = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(base_path, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, device_map=\"cuda:0\", trust_remote_code=True)\nif adapter_path:\n    model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, adapter_path)\n    model = model.merge_and_unload()\nmodel.eval()\n```\n\nYes/no token extraction：\n```python\ndef get_yes_no_ids(tokenizer):\n    yes_ids, no_ids = [], []\n    for w in [\"yes\", \"Yes\", \"YES\"]:\n        ids = tokenizer.encode(w, add_special_tokens=False)\n        if len(ids) == 1: yes_ids.append(ids[0])\n    for w in [\"no\", \"No\", \"NO\"]:\n        ids = tokenizer.encode(w, add_special_tokens=False)\n        if len(ids) == 1: no_ids.append(ids[0])\n    return list(set(yes_ids)), list(set(no_ids))\n```\n\nBatch verification (forward pass, get logits at last position, softmax over yes/no tokens):\n```python\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef batch_verify(model, tokenizer, prompts, yes_ids, no_ids, batch_size=16, device=\"cuda\"):\n    # ... tokenize, forward, extract logits at last token, softmax over yes+no, return P(yes)\n```\n\n评估函数：\n```python\ndef entity_set(entities):\n    return {(e[\"start\"], e[\"end\"], e[\"type\"]) for e in entities}\n\ndef compute_prf(pred, gold):\n    if not gold and not pred: return 1.0, 1.0, 1.0\n    if not pred or not gold: return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0\n    tp = len(pred & gold)\n    if tp == 0: return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0\n    p, r = tp/len(pred), tp/len(gold)\n    return p, r, 2*p*r/(p+r)\n```\n\n**Step 3: Entity-level mean-pooled hidden state representations enable latent aggregation (centroid selection, latent confidence scoring, clustering) that bypasses LP compression and provides discriminative per-sample ranking unavailable from output-space signals.\n- **Parent**: exp_e2_mixture_of_signals\n\n### 你要做什么\n\n**Step 1: 探查 autodl-5090-c 环境**（ssh_read_only）\n\n```bash\nsource /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output -name \"samples*.jsonl\" 2>/dev/null\nls -la /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/output/ 2>/dev/null\n\n# 可用模型\nls -la samples（JSONL，每行 instance with `text`, `samples`[each with `entities`], `gold`, `greedy`）\n\n2. **Forward pass 提取 hidden states**：\n   - 对每个 sample，构建完整输入：chat template(prompt + generated output)\n   - Forward pass，提取最后一层 hidden states\n   - 只保留 generated output 部分的 hidden states（不要 prompt 部分）\n\n3. **Entity span 定位 + Mean Pooling**：\n   - 在 generated output token 序列中，找到每个 entity text 的 token 位置\n   - 方法：tokenize generated output with return_offsets_mapping，用 char offset matching 找 entity text span\n   - 对 entity 的 token range 做 mean pooling → entity hidden vector (dim = hidden_size, e.g. 3584 for 7B)\n   - **Fallback**: 如果 offset mapping 失败，用 entity text 单独 encode 的 token ids 在 hidden states 序列中做 sliding window match\n\n4. **三种 Latent Aggregation 方法**：\n\n   **A) Centroid Selection**（sample-level）:\n   - 对每个 instance，每个 sample 的 \"latent fingerprint\" = mean of all entity hidden vectors in that sample\n   - Instance centroid = mean of all samples' fingerprints\n   - 选与 centroid cosine similarity 最高的 sample\n   - → selection F1\n\n   **B) Latent Entity Confidence**（entity-level construction）:\n   - 对每个 unique entity across samples，收集它在不同 samples 中的 hidden vectors\n   - Latent confidence = mean pairwise cosine similarity of these vectors（高 = 模型一致性高）\n   - 用 latent confidence 做 entity-level weighted construction（类似 ESJ，但用 hidden state consistency 代替 P(yes)）\n   - Sweep thresholds for inclusion\n\n   **C) LP vs Latent comparison**（诊断）:\n   - 对每个 instance，计算 LP-based sample ranking 和 latent centroid-based ranking 的 Spearman ρ\n   - 如果低 → latent 提供 LP 之外的新信息\n   - 记录 latent selection 改善了哪些 LP 失败的 cases\n\n5. **显存管理**（关键！）:\n   - 逐 instance 处理：加载一个 instance 的所有 samples → forward pass → 提取 hidden states → entity pooling → 聚合 → 释放\n   - 不缓存所有 hidden states\n   - batch_size 设小（默认 2-4）因为需要 output_hidden_states=True\n\n6. **Baselines**: greedy F1, majority vote (sweep θ), LP selection F1, oracle F1\n\n7. **输出**: lsc_results.json + summary\n\n脚本参数：\n```\n--base_model_path\n--adapter_path       # 可选\n--data_path          # inference samples JSONL\n--output_dir\n--hidden_layer       # 默认 -1（最后一层）\n--batch_size         # 默认 2（hidden states 占显存大）\n--max_instances      # 默认 0\n```\n\n**关键代码模式**：\n\n模型加载（需要 output_hidden_states）：\n```python\nmodel = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n    base_path, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, \n    device_map=\"cuda:0\",  # 始终 cuda:0，框架处理物理 GPU 映射\n    trust_remote_code=True,\n    output_hidden_states=True,  # 关键！\n)\n```\n\nHidden state extraction：\n```python\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef extract_hidden_states(model, tokenizer, texts, batch_size=2, layer=-1, device=\"cuda\"):\n    \"\"\"Forward pass, return last-layer hidden states for each text.\"\"\"\n    all_hidden = []\n    for i in range(0, len(texts), batch_size):\n        batch = texts[i:i+batch_size]\n        inputs = tokenizer(batch, return_tensors=\"pt\", padding=True, truncation=True, max_length=2048).to(device)\n        outputs = model(**inputs, output_hidden_states=True)\n        hidden = outputs.hidden_states[layer]  # (B, L, H)\n        for j in range(len(batch)):\n            seq_len = inputs.attention_mask[j].sum()\n            all_hidden.append(hidden[j, :seq_len].cpu())  # 立即移到 CPU 省显存\n        del outputs, hidden\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n    return all_hidden\n```\n\nEntity span matching：\n```python\ndef find_entity_token_spans(tokenizer, generated_text, entities):\n    \"\"\"Map entity text spans to token indices in generated_text.\"\"\"\n    encoding = tokenizer(generated_text, return_offsets_mapping=True, add_special_tokens=False)\n    offsets = encoding['offset_mapping']\n    spans = {}\n    for e in entities:\n        # Find char start/end of entity text in generated_text\n        char_start = generated_text.find(e['text'])\n        if char_start == -1:\n            continue\n        char_end = char_start + len(e['text'])\n        # Map to token indices\n        tok_start, tok_end = None, None\n        for ti, (cs, ce) in enumerate(offsets):\n            if cs <= char_start < ce and tok_start is None:\n                tok_start = ti\n            if cs < char_end <= ce:\n                tok_end = ti + 1\n                break\n        if tok_start is not None and tok_end is not None:\n            spans[(e['start'], e['end'], e['type'])] = (tok_start, tok_end)\n    return spans\n```\n\n评估函数（与 ESJ 相同）：\n```python\ndef entity_set(entities):\n    return {(e[\"start\"], e[\"end\"], e[\"type\"]) for e in entities}\n\ndef compute_prf(pred, gold):\n    if not gold and not pred: return 1.0, 1.0, 1.0\n    if not pred or not gold: return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0\n    tp = len(pred & gold)\n    if tp == 0: return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0\n    p, r = tp/len(pred), tp/len(gold)\n    return p, r, 2*p*r/(p+r)\n```\n\n**Step 3: 选择模型和数据**\n\n探查后确定：\n- 使用与生成 FewNERD inference samples 的同一模型\n- 从 samples JSONL 的目录名 / metadata 推断模型身份\n\n**Step 4: Dry run**\n\n```bash\nCUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=2 python /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/code/run_lsc.py \\\n  --base_model_path &lt;MODEL_PATH>
